;(function(win, doc, style, timeout) {
  var STYLE_ID = 'at-body-style';

  function getParent() {
    return doc.getElementsByTagName('head')[0];
  }

  function addStyle(parent, id, def) {
    if (!parent) {
      return;
    }

    var style = doc.createElement('style');
    style.id = id;
    style.innerHTML = def;
    parent.appendChild(style);
  }

  function removeStyle(parent, id) {
    if (!parent) {
      return;
    }

    var style = doc.getElementById(id);

    if (!style) {
      return;
    }

    parent.removeChild(style);
  }

  addStyle(getParent(), STYLE_ID, style);
  setTimeout(function() {
    removeStyle(getParent(), STYLE_ID);
  }, timeout);
}(window, document, "body {opacity: 0 !important}", 3000));
</script>

    <div class="richtext text cmp-top-spacing-84 cmp-bottom-spacing-32 aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12">
<div data-icon-aria-label=" (opens in a new tab)" id="richtext-6297b53bb8" class="cmp-text rich-text-container">
    <div class="rich-text-wrapper">
        <h2 class="richtext--element-font"><span class="variant headlineLarge"><b><span class="color brand_color">Frequently asked questions</span></b></span></h2>
 
        <!-- Used unsafe context as temporary fix and create adobe support ticket to
        figure out why html context adding extra ul and li elements -->
    </div>
</div>

    





    

</div>
<div class="accordion cmp-bottom-spacing-48 aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12">
<div>
    <dig-accordion data-cmp-multiple-expansion>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_1406572627_accordionItems_item0">
                What is CoCounsel Legal? 
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_1406572627_accordionItems_item0">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>We’ve engineered CoCounsel Legal to work at the level of a senior associate, reasoning through legal issues the same way an attorney does — from the sources you already trust. It is the only legal AI that reasons from authoritative Westlaw primary law, trusted Practical Law guidance, and your organization&#39;s knowledge. Built to meet the standard the profession demands, it is Fiduciary-Grade AI that attorneys can trust with the work that matters.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_1406572627_accordionItems_item1">
                How do I use CoCounsel Legal? 
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_1406572627_accordionItems_item1">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>Describe your matter in plain language — whether it is a contract review, litigation strategy, deal structuring, or circuit split analysis — and CoCounsel Legal handles the rest. It develops the legal approach, validates the work, and produces high-quality work product. Every step of the reasoning is visible as it works, and every citation is linked so attorneys can click through and verify the work themselves.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_1406572627_accordionItems_item2">
                Does CoCounsel Legal use AI?  
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_1406572627_accordionItems_item2">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>Yes, CoCounsel Legal uses generative AI and agentic AI to handle complex legal work and power capabilities like deep research and litigation document analysis. Grounded in trusted Westlaw and Practical Law content, CoCounsel Legal provides verifiable results, so you can feel confident your work is thurough and accurate.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_1406572627_accordionItems_item3">
                What does &#39;engineered to work at the level of a senior associate&#39; mean?

                    <div slot="content">

                            <p>CoCounsel Legal is designed to handle complex, multistep legal work the same way an experienced attorney would — developing the right legal approach, working through each step, and delivering polished work product with citations.</p>

                <script type="text/javascript">
// Hide OneTrust banner if wcmmode=disabled is present in URL or in PROD preview domain
if (window.location.search.includes('wcmmode=disabled') || window.location.host.includes('preview-prod-cs.ewp.thomsonreuters.com')) {
    const style = document.createElement('style');
    style.textContent = '[id*="onetrust"],[class*="onetrust"],[id*="ot-"],[class*="ot-"]{display:none!important}';
    document.head.appendChild(style);
}

// function to pull cookie value
function getCookie(name) {
    var value = "; " + document.cookie;
    var parts = value.split("; " + name + "=");
    if (parts.length == 2) return parts.pop().split(";").shift();
}
function OptanonWrapper() { 
        setTimeout(() => {
            _satellite.pageBottom();
        }, 1000);
    var OABCcookieName = "OptanonConsent";
    var bannerAcceptBtn = document.getElementById("onetrust-accept-btn-handler");
    var pcAllowAllBtn = document.getElementById("accept-recommended-btn-handler");
    var pcSaveBtn = document.getElementsByClassName("save-preference-btn-handler onetrust-close-btn-handler")[0];
    var OABCcookie = getCookie(OABCcookieName);
        
   
    // IF logic needed here because ot-banner-sdk DIV is not injected on page loads if banner is not exposed
    if (!OABCcookie && bannerAcceptBtn) {
        bannerAcceptBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            location.reload();
        });
    }
    if (pcAllowAllBtn)
        pcAllowAllBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            location.reload();
        });
    if(pcSaveBtn){
        pcSaveBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            setTimeout(()=> {
            location.reload();
        }, 1000) //quick timeout so that the consent receipt can be sent and the cookie can be updated
        });
    }
} </script>
